<h3 class="awr">Background Wait Events</h3>
<ul>
<li class="awr"> ordered by wait time desc, waits desc (idle events last) </li>
<li class="awr"> Only events with Total Wait Time (s) &gt;= .001 are shown </li>
<li class="awr"> %Timeouts: value of 0 indicates value was &lt; .5%.  Value of null is truly 0</li>

<li class="awr"> %CPU   - CPU Time      as a percentage of Elapsed Time </li>
<li class="awr"> %IO    - User I/O Time as a percentage of Elapsed Time</li>

ription = 'MICRO' THEN 'PENDIENTE' ELSE 'DUDOSO' END AS RESULTADO_NORM,  r.firstreceptiondate as Recibida,  to_char(r.requestdate,  'dd/mm/yyyy') as FECHA,  r.requestlabel as SID,  r.requestid as SID_NEW,  REPLACE(r.ORDERPLACERNUMBER,  'SGP') as SIDPLACER,  UPPER(s.servicedescription) as SERVICIO_PETICIONARIO,  UPPER(c.centerdescription) as HOSPITAL_PETICIONARIO,  UPPER(p.patientname) as NOMBRE,  UPPER(p.patientsurname) as APELLIDO_1,  to_char(p.dob ,  'dd/mm/yyyy') as FECHA_NAC,  UPPER(p.IDENTITYCARD) as DNI,  UPPER(p.ssn) as NSS,  UPPER(p.externalid) as NHCHis,  UPPER(p.gender) as SEXO,  UPPER(p.address) DIRECCION,  UPPER(p.city) AS CIUDAD,  UPPER(p.state) AS PROVINCIA,  UPPER(p.country) AS PAIS,  UPPER(p.postalcode) COD_POSTAL ,  UPPER(p.phone) AS TELEFONO ,  --UPPER(p.cip) AS CIP,  CASE WHEN p.nts IS NULL THEN 'VACIO' ELSE p.nts END as CIA ,  CASE WHEN r.iscomplete = 'N' THEN 'INCOMPLETO' ELSE 'COMPLETO' END AS STATUS,  rt.resultdate as FECHA_RESULTADO,  rt.isrequestcomplete AS REQ

DECLARE V_IP VARCHAR2(20); v_instance_number number; v_machinename v$session.machine%type; v_osuser v$session.osuser%type; v_dbuser v$session.username%type; v_type v$session.type%type; v_service_name v$session.service_name%type; v_program varchar2(50); v_module varchar2(50); v_sesion_sid number; v_sesion_serial number; v_idlog number; v_check number := 1; --0: pasa. 1:no pasa v_out_check number; v_out_ses varchar2(1000); v_out_apl varchar2(1000); v_nivel_log number; v_server_host varchar2(1000); v_profile varchar2(30); v_rcgroup varchar2(32); v_admindba varchar2(1) := 'N'; BEGIN -- Obtencion de constantes V_IP := SYS_CONTEXT('USERENV',  'IP_ADDRESS'); v_instance_number := SYS_CONTEXT('USERENV',  'INSTANCE'); v_server_host := upper(SYS_CONTEXT('USERENV',  'SERVER_HOST')); SELECT valor into v_nivel_log from acc_admin.t_parametros where keyid=10003; BEGIN DBMS_SESSION.SET_IDENTIFIER('#IP'||V_IP||'#'||USER); EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN DBMS_SESSION.SET_IDENTIFIER(USER); END; -- Se obtiene t
oda la informacion de la sesion SELECT sid,  serial#,  UPPER(machine),  UPPER(username),  upper(type),  upper(service_name),  UPPER(program),  UPPER(module),  upper(osuser),  upper(resource_consumer_group) INTO v_sesion_sid,  v_sesion_serial,  v_machinename,  v_dbuser,  v_type,  v_service_name,  v_program,  v_module,  v_osuser,  v_rcgroup FROM v$session WHERE audsid = userenv('sessionid') AND rownum &lt; 2; -- NO SE MUEVE LA SESION,  LO METERA EN OTHERS if (v_service_name = 'SYS$USERS')and(v_server_host = v_machinename) then if v_nivel_log = 2 then insert into acc_admin.t_mods_logon_log (idlog, logon_date, servicio, os_usuario, ora_usuario, maquina, ip, programa, modulo, origen, check_in, check_in_sess, check_in_aplic) values(acc_admin.S_GES_MODS_POSTCHECK.nextval, sysdate, v_service_name, v_osuser, v_dbuser, v_machinename, v_ip, v_program, v_module, 'EXITO_TRIGGER', '0', 'SYS$USERS', 'SYS$USERS'); commit; end if; return; end if; if v_type = 'USER' then -- Usuarios autorizados con el A
DMINDBA = S en la t_mods_sesion begin select 0,  'S' into v_check,  v_admindba from acc_admin.t_mods_sesion where (ora_usuario = v_dbuser or maquina = v_machinename) and admindba = 'S'; exception when others then v_check := '1'; v_admindba := 'N'; end; if v_check = 1 then insert into acc_admin.t_mods_temp_sesion(idlog, servicio, os_usuario, ora_usuario, maquina, ip, programa, modulo) values(1,  v_service_name,  v_osuser,  v_dbuser,  v_machinename,  V_IP,  v_program,  v_module); -- Se comprueba si la sesion,  el usuario,  servicio,  etc son correctos v_check := acc_admin.FUNC_MODS_CHECK_SERVICES(1,  v_out_check,  v_out_ses,  v_out_apl); end if; -- Si todo es correcto se registra la entrada y se deja entrar if v_check = 0 then if v_nivel_log = 2 then insert into acc_admin.t_mods_logon_log (idlog, logon_date, servicio, os_usuario, ora_usuario, maquina, ip, programa, modulo, origen, check_in, check_in_sess, check_in_aplic) values(acc_admin.S_GES_MODS_POSTCHECK.nextval, sysdate, v_service_n
ame, v_osuser, v_dbuser, v_machinename, v_ip, v_program, v_module, 'EXITO_TRIGGER', v_out_check, v_out_ses, v_out_apl); end if; if v_admindba = 'N' then insert into acc_admin.t_mods_postcheck_log (idlog, logon_date, instance_number, sesion_sid, sesion_serial, servicio, os_usuario, ora_usuario, maquina, ip, programa, modulo) values(acc_admin.S_GES_MODS_POSTCHECK.nextval, sysdate, v_instance_number, v_sesion_sid, v_sesion_serial, v_service_name, v_osuser, v_dbuser, v_machinename, v_ip, v_program, v_module); end if; commit; -- Se obtiene el perfil del usuario select p.name into v_profile from user$ u,  profname$ p where u.resource$ = p.profile# and u.name = v_dbuser; if (v_profile = 'NOMINALES' or v_profile like 'POOL%' or v_profile like 'CREADORES%' or v_profile = 'EVERIS' or v_profile = 'CAU') then if v_rcgroup = 'OTHER_GROUPS' then BEGIN DBMS_RESOURCE_MANAGER.SWITCH_CONSUMER_GROUP_FOR_SESS ( session_id =&gt; v_sesion_sid,  session_serial =&gt; v_sesion_serial,  consumer_group =&gt; 'DG
A_OLTP'); END; end if; end if; end if; -- Si no le esta permitida la entrada se registra el intento y se lanza excepcion if v_check = 1 then if v_nivel_log &gt; 0 then insert into acc_admin.t_mods_logon_log (idlog, logon_date, servicio, os_usuario, ora_usuario, maquina, ip, programa, modulo, origen) values(acc_admin.S_GES_MODS_POSTCHECK.nextval, sysdate, v_service_name, v_osuser, v_dbuser, v_machinename, v_ip, v_program, v_module, 'TRIGGER') returning idlog into v_idlog; commit; end if; raise_application_error(-20001, 'Apertura de sesion no autorizada en el servicio solicitado. Contacte con el equipo de administracion. Suministre IDLOG: '||v_idlog); end if; end if; END;</td></tr>

select HOSPITAL,  RESULTADO, RESULTADO_NORM, RECIBIDA, FECHA, SID, SID_NEW,  SERVICIO_PETICIONARIO,  HOSPITAL_PETICIONARIO, NOMBRE,  APELLIDO_1,  FECHA_NAC, DNI, NSS, NHCHis, SEXO, CIUDAD,  PROVINCIA,  PAIS,  COD_POSTAL, TELEFONO, CIA, FECHA_RESULTADO, REQUEST_COMPLETE from( select :&quot;SYS_B_00&quot; AS HOSPITAL, v.shortdescription AS RESULTADO, CASE WHEN v.shortdescription = :&quot;SYS_B_01&quot; THEN :&quot;SYS_B_02&quot; WHEN v.shortdescription = :&quot;SYS_B_03&quot; THEN :&quot;SYS_B_04&quot; WHEN v.shortdescription = :&quot;SYS_B_05&quot; THEN :&quot;SYS_B_06&quot; WHEN v.shortdescription = :&quot;SYS_B_07&quot; THEN :&quot;SYS_B_08&quot; WHEN v.shortdescription = :&quot;SYS_B_09&quot; THEN :&quot;SYS_B_10&quot; ELSE :&quot;SYS_B_11&quot; END AS RESULTADO_NORM,  r.firstreceptiondate as Recibida, to_char(r.requestdate,  :&quot;SYS_B_12&quot;) as FECHA, r.requestlabel as SID, r.requestid as SID_NEW, UPPER(s.servicedescription) as SERVICIO_PETICIONARIO,  UPPER(c.centerdescription
) as HOSPITAL_PETICIONARIO, UPPER(p.patientname) as NOMBRE, UPPER(p.patientsurname) as APELLIDO_1, to_char(p.dob ,  :&quot;SYS_B_13&quot;) as FECHA_NAC,  UPPER(p.IDENTITYCARD) as DNI, UPPER(p.ssn) as NSS, UPPER(p.externalid) as NHCHis, UPPER(p.gender) as SEXO, UPPER(p.address) DIRECCION, UPPER(p.city) AS CIUDAD, UPPER(p.state) AS PROVINCIA,  UPPER(p.country) AS PAIS, UPPER(p.postalcode) COD_POSTAL , UPPER(p.phone) AS TELEFONO , CASE WHEN p.nts IS NULL THEN :&quot;SYS_B_14&quot; ELSE p.nts END as CIA ,  CASE WHEN r.iscomplete = :&quot;SYS_B_15&quot; THEN :&quot;SYS_B_16&quot; ELSE :&quot;SYS_B_17&quot; END AS STATUS, rt.resultdate as FECHA_RESULTADO, rt.isrequestcomplete AS REQUEST_COMPLETE FROM MGOLD_OWN.resultcodedvaluefinal v, MGOLD_OWN.request r,  MGOLD_OWN.patient p,  MGOLD_OWN.service s,  MGOLD_OWN.center c,  MGOLD_OWN.requesttest rt where p.nts=:&quot;SYS_B_18&quot; and RT.ISREJECTED = :&quot;SYS_B_19&quot; and R.ISPREREQUEST = :&quot;SYS_B_20&quot; and R.ISCANCELLED = :&quot;SYS
_B_21&quot; and r.requestid = v.requestid and r.requestid = rt.requestid and rt.testid = v.testid and p.patientid = r.patientid and s.serviceid = r.serviceid and c.centerid = s.centerid and rt.testid = :&quot;SYS_B_22&quot; and RT.RESULTSTATUS IN (:&quot;SYS_B_23&quot;, :&quot;SYS_B_24&quot;) and r.requestdate &gt; sysdate - interval :&quot;SYS_B_25&quot; day order by recibida desc) where rownum&lt;:&quot;SYS_B_26&quot;</td></tr>

<h3 class="awr">Shared Pool Advisory</h3>
<ul>
<li class="awr"> SP: Shared Pool     Est LC: Estimated Library Cache   Factr: Factor </li>
<li class="awr"> Note there is often a 1:Many correlation between a single logical object    in the Library Cache, and the physical number of memory objects associated    with it.  Therefore comparing the number of Lib Cache objects (e.g. in     v$librarycache), with the number of Lib Cache Memory Objects is invalid.</li>
</ul>

<h3 class="awr">Process Memory Summary</h3>
<ul>
<li class="awr"> B: Begin Snap   E: End Snap  </li>
<li class="awr"> All rows below contain absolute values (i.e. not diffed over the interval) </li>
<li class="awr"> Max Alloc is Maximum PGA Allocation size at snapshot time  </li>

<h2 class="awr">
Global Cache Transfer Stats
</h2>
<ul>
<li class="awr"><a class="awr" href="#5400">Global Cache Transfer Stats</a></li>
<li class="awr"><a class="awr" href="#5450">Global Cache Transfer Times (ms)</a></li>
<li class="awr"><a class="awr" href="#5460">Global Cache Transfer (Immediate)</a></li>

</ul>
<a class="awr" href="#top">Back to Top</a><p />
<a class="awr" name="5400"></a>
<h3 class="awr">Global Cache Transfer Stats</h3>
<ul>
<li class="awr"> Immediate  (Immed) - Block Transfer NOT impacted by Remote Processing Delays </li>
<li class="awr"> Busy        (Busy) - Block Transfer impacted by Remote Contention </li>
<li class="awr"> Congested (Congst) - Block Transfer impacted by Remote System Load </li>
<li class="awr"> ordered by CR + Current Blocks Received desc</li>
</ul>

<h3 class="awr">Interconnect Ping Latency Stats</h3>
<ul>
<li class="awr"> Ping latency of the roundtrip of a message from this instance to </li>
<li class="awr"> target instances.  </li>
<li class="awr"> The target instance is identified by an instance number. </li>
<li class="awr"> Average and standard deviation of ping latency is given in miliseconds </li>
<li class="awr"> for message sizes of 500 bytes and 8K. </li>
<li class="awr"> Note that latency of a message from the instance to itself is used as </li>
<li class="awr"> control, since message latency can include wait for CPU</li>
</ul>
